中砂 (1560 TT)  Trading Buy

先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，投資建議
給 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，中
砂持續擴充鑽石碟的產能，帶動獲利、評價提升，投資建議給
Trading Buy 。  
中砂 2Q26 獲利大幅年增： 中砂 2Q26 營收 24.93 億元，
QoQ+8.70%/YoY+17.87% ；稅後純益 5.23 億元，
QoQ+19.94%/YoY+96.49% ；稅後 EPS 3.46 元。 2Q26 業外利益
包含投資獲利。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況： ABU：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂
輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於 2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨
持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出貨。
SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望： 傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ：2026 年將
受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目標營收較 2025 雙位
數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐
季持續增加，美系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率
及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ：2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，
高階產品組合比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。  
群益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元：群
益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 101.87 億元 /135.79 億元，
YoY+25.00%/+33.30% ；稅後純益 21.97 億元 /31.87 億元，
YoY+61.48%/+45.07% ；稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E：(Environmental) 。中砂秉持企業經營須兼顧環境保護之理念，持續落實環境
友善管理策略與措施。國際永續趨勢與國家 2050 淨零路徑推動下，中砂透過
執行組織型溫室氣體盤查、產品碳足跡盤查及導入循環經濟專案，循序漸進達
成減碳目標。  
此外，中砂重視氣候變遷伴隨之風險與機會，亦將氣候行動納入公司風險管理
與營運策略，積極研擬因應策略及應變措施；對於水資源、空氣污染、廢棄物
等環境議題，更推行各項管理與減量方案，朝向永續經營之願景邁進。  
S： (Social) 。中砂長期深耕地方社區創生與投入藝文教育與活動，使參與社會
公益成為中砂的企業文化。秉持著「共好：你好、我好、大家好」的經營理念
及懷著「知恩、感恩、報恩」的心，中砂持續與當地縣市政府、學校、社福機
構團體與自身基金會合作，攜手保留珍貴產業文化，提倡書法教育，為當地社
區帶來實質性的協助與回饋，發揮企業正向影響力。  
中砂座落於台灣著名的陶瓷之鄉 — 鶯歌，期望與鄉里共榮，為社區民眾打造友
善的生活環境，因此長期參與社區關懷與愛鄉感恩公益，連續多年贊助新北市
鶯歌區西鶯里、中湖里的敬老活動與銀髮族共餐活動，並致贈年節禮盒及物資
予鄰里長輩。近年中砂更與民間公益培訓單位合作，透過「學生提案，企業協
力」的方式，與公益團體合作推出聯名禮盒、愛心送餐等創新專案。除了達到
支援公益團體的目的外，中砂更將聯名禮盒分送給鶯歌的鄰里長輩，持續傳遞
公益善心。  
G： (Governance) 。企業永續發展的關鍵建基在健全且完善的公司治理架構上，
使得強化董事會監督管理職能成為中砂精進公 司治理的核心目標。除了保障股
東權益外，中砂更積極建構具備應對傳統及氣候變遷、資訊安全等新興風險的
管理機制，透過完整的鑑別、評估及擬定因應措施的流程，降低潛在風險對公
司營運之衝擊。在持續提升營運韌性的同時，也為利害關係人創造更大的價值。
公司簡介：  
中國砂輪股份有限公司成立於 07/1964 ，總部位於鶯歌，主要從事生產及銷售
砂輪、鑽石碟、再生晶圓等研磨工具。  
公司早期產品以應用於石材加工的傳統砂輪為主，後因取得鑽石碟技術的專利
授權及台系大客戶供應鏈在地化策略，以鑽石碟產品切入半導體產業領域。
2020 年，公司再生晶圓事業部竹科廠於竹南科 學園區落成啟用量產，成為全球
前三大再生晶圓供應商。  
再生晶圓主要台灣同業包含辛耘 (3583 TT) 、昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) ；鑽石碟為
3M(MMM US) 、Abrasive Technology 、Asahi(4216 JP) 、Seasol( 韓 )等。中砂大客
戶包含台系晶圓代工廠、美系記憶體廠等。  
中砂自 04/2025 開始認列日本三井金屬礦業集團旗下的兩家公司 MKS 與 MGT，
歸類在傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) 。  
營收占比：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ，毛利率 20~25% 。用於工具機、機械，近期還有包含半
導體 (碳化矽 )的產業。

3 群益投顧 
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ，毛利率 45% 以上。用於成熟製程、先進製程、先進封裝。
單一客戶營收占比超過 54% 。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ，毛利率 30~35% 。用於半導體製程，單價約 10~20 美金。

資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況：  
ABU ：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於
2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出
貨。 SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
2026 年 CAPEX 與擴產計畫：  
中砂 2026 年 CAPEX 約 15 億元， 4Q26 鑽石碟月產能預計增加至 7.0 萬顆、
4Q27 增加至 9.5 萬顆； 4Q26 12 吋再生晶圓月產能將增加至 40 萬片， 8 吋約
15 萬片，共 55 萬片。  
資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ： 2026 年將受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目
標營收較 2025 雙位數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐季持續增加，美
系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ： 2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，高階產品組合
比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2368金像電 
(Gold Circuit Electronics)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/22 研 究 員： 方姿婷  (Lesley  Fang ) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要：  
⚫ 2Q26 營收 符 合 預 期，毛 利 率 受人 民 幣 升 值 匯 率 干 擾、直 接 人 工
成 本 增 加 影 響，毛 利 率 略 低 於35%，展 望 3Q26 毛 利 率 應 可 回 歸
常態增至 37~38% 之間 。 
⚫ AI 伺 服 器 帶 動 高 階 銅 箔 基 板與 PCB(HDI 、HLC) 厚板 需 求 強 勁，
2H26 ASIC 客戶 (AWS Trainium 4 及 Google TPU V9) 等，預計採
用 HDI 與 HLC 混 壓 設 計 ， 金 像 電 於3~4 階 HDI 混壓技術已經
相 對 純 熟 可 順 利 供 應 。 
⚫ 2H26 至 2027 年， CCL /PCB 產 業 升 級 朝 向1.6T Switch 發 展 ， 預
期 板 層 數 提 升 至44~52L 、CCL 材 料 端 為 M8 或 M9 等 級 供 應 ，
對 於 金 像 電 而 言 ，PCB 板 層 數 提 升 幅 度 達15~20% 。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報 符合市場預期， 3Q26 展望正向  
金像電 2Q26 營收 242.79 億元， +25.71%QoQ ，+75.26%YoY ， 毛 利
率 34.65% ，稅 後 淨 利 47.83 億 元，稅 後 EPS+9.24 元。對於 2H26 看
法，因 應 客 戶 強 勁 需 求(AI ASIC 厚板 )，產 能 擴 充 方 面，新 增 蘇 州、
泰國新廠 HDI 厚 板 支 應。展望 3Q26 ，累計 7~8 月 份 金 像 電 營 收 達
成率 67% 符 合 市 場 預 期 ， 預 估 金 像 電 營 收 為 305.86 億 元 ，
+25.98%QoQ ， 毛 利 率 37.55% ， 稅 後 淨 利 67.04 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後
EPS+12.72 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 積極  
宏遠投顧觀察 ，AI 伺 服 器 與 先 進 封 裝 等 需 求 日 增 ，同 時 對 於 一 般
型 伺 服 器 用 板、高 階Switch 用 板、AI 伺 服 器 用 板，需 求 迅 速 拉 升。
回顧近年來 ，金 像 電 產 能 擴 充，各 廠 區 台 灣 廠30 億 元、蘇 州 廠 38
億 元、常 熟 一 廠 10 億 元、常 熟 二 廠 3 億 元，2H26 ASIC 客戶 (AWS 
Trainium 4 及 Google TPU V9) 等，預 計 採 用 HDI 與 HLC 混 壓 設 計，
金像電於 3~4 階 HDI 混 壓 技 術 已 經 相 對 純 熟 ， 對 於2H26~2027 年
未 來 訂 單 能 見 度 影 響 不 大， 未 來 出 貨 將 可 順 利 供 貨。2027 年隨著
NVIDIA  Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材 料 出 貨 攀 升 ，AI ASIC 
CCL 拉 貨 動 能呈現 50~60% 高 度 成 長 ，CCL 、PCB 產 品 組 合 持 續 優
化。2027 年 起，CCL 與 PCB 產 業 產 品 升 級，除一般型伺服器 Intel 
Diamond Rapids 平 台 、AMD Zen6 Venice 平 台 採 用PCIe Gen6 與 Ultra 
Low Loss CCL(M7) ，PCB 板層數增至 20~24L ；2027 年 ， 隨 著1.6T

三. 金像 電對標高階 Switch 板材， 2027 年為成長主軸  
金像電 於 AI 伺 服 器 與新 平 台之 間 設 計 資 料 傳 輸 速 率、資 料 交 換 與
處 理 等 級 提 升 ， 對 於 高 階Switch 網 路 交 換 器 需 求 同 步 增 加 。觀察
AI 產 業 發 展，高 階Switch 於 2026 年 已 全 面 升 級 至800GSwitch ，金
像電 過往 為 品 牌(企業用 Switch )與 白 牌 主 要 交 換 器PCB 供 應 商 ，
以 800G Switch 為 例，PCB 板層數 38~44L 設 計、CCL 材料端為 M8
等 級，金 像 電 營 收 網 通 產 品 已 達10~20% 當 中 多 數 為 高 階Switch 板
材，展 望 2027 年 CCL/PCB 產 業 升 級 朝 向1.6T Switch 發 展，預 期 板
層數提升至 44~52L 、CCL 材 料 端 為 M8 或 M9 等級 供 應 ， 對 於 金
像電而言， PCB 板 層 數 提 升 幅 度 達15~20% 。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 43.68 /80.57 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺 服 器及 ASIC 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ，金像 電為
AI PCB 領 導 廠 商與 AI ASIC 厚 板 主 要 供 甕 商， 將 受 惠AI Server 對
於高 階 厚 板 與HLC 板 材、HDI 板材 強 勁 的 需 求。因而 ，預估 2026
年 金像 電 營 收 為1,086 .36 億元， +81.04%YoY ， 毛 利 率36.75%，稅
後淨利 228 .27 億 元，稅 後 EPS+ 43.68 元。展 望 2027 年，在 AI 伺服
器 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ， 帶 動金像電 PCB 產能 持續 增長， 預估
2027 年 金像 電營收為 1,749 .83 億元， +61.07%YoY ，毛利率為
40.01%， 稅 後 淨 利424.59 億元，稅後 EPS+ 80.57 元。

四.投資建議維持 買進，目標價 1,290 元 
宏 遠 投 顧 認 為，金像 電 營 運 重 點 在 於，營 運 動 能來 自 於一 般 型 伺 服
器 CPU 廠商 Intel、AMD 均 有 新 品 帶 動 新 平 台 升 級 與 相 關 供 應 的 零
組 件 商 機，以 及 攻 入AI 伺 服 器、高 階 網 路 交 換 器與 一 般 型 伺 服 器
主板 規 格 轉 換 成 長 可 期。基 於 上 述，給 予金像 電 投 資 建 議 為 買 進，
目標價 1,290 元(16.00x 20 27 EPS) 。

1.HDI 與 M8 以 上 升 級 仍 為 關 注 焦 點 ，2026~2028 年 產 能 建 置 積 極 
過 往 一 般 型 伺 服 器 用 板 採用 HDI 設計 ， 強 調 細 線 路 設 計 ，CPU 尺
寸變大 Pin C ount 數 量 增 加 至2,000 只~4,000 只 Pin Count ，PCB 板
面積不變之下要走 向 細 線 路化(線寬 /線距 )朝 向 更 細 線 路 設 計 。 製
程上 HDI 經 過 多 次 壓 合 後，PP 在 熱 壓 多 次後，每 一 層 熱 膨 脹 係 數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相 對 具 備 生 產 優 勢 與 競 爭 力 ， 相 對 應 台 光 電 、 台 燿 等 高
階 CCL 供 貨 與 升 級 也 成 為 製 程 一 大 瓶 頸 ；回 顧 近 年 來 ， 金 像 電 產
能 擴 充 ， 各 廠 區 台 灣 廠30 億 元 、 蘇 州 廠38 億 元 、 常 熟 一 廠10 億

王淑君 
Jenny.wang@ctbcsis.com 
◎研究分析標的公司為本公司關係企業 
之客戶。

盟立 (2464 TT)

重新覆蓋  
買進(Buy) 
產能擴張推升 2027 成長，機器人接棒第二曲線  
⚫ 在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高  
⚫ 半導體需求強勁 ，產能同步擴張  
⚫ 機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期  
在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高 ：盟立 2025 年營收 66.81 億
元，毛利率 18.8% ，EPS -0.8 元，近年持續投入新客戶 與 新 專 案 開 發，
為後續擴產需求預作布局。進入 2026 年後營運逐步轉強， 2Q26 營收
21.69 億元 (QoQ +16% 、YoY +7.9%)， 毛利率 22.2%，EPS 0.42 元，1H26
轉虧為盈，主要受惠先進封裝擴產，帶動 OHT、AGV 及 Stocker 等產
品成長。截至 8 月累計接單約 140 億元，其中半導體占 75% 、智能物
流 10%~15% ，年底訂單有望接近 200 億元，且 75%~80% 可望於 2027
年底前完成， 預期今年營收呈現逐月、逐季提升趨勢 。 
半導體需求強勁，產能同步擴張 ：半導體仍是盟立未來最重要的成長
核心，公司目前聚焦先進封裝、高階載板及半導體自動化三大領域，
部分客戶擴產規劃已延伸至 2028~2029 年，部分載板客戶既有擴產仍
不足以滿足需求，持續尋找新廠房與土地。此外， 公司產品技術已具
備進入 Fab 的條件 ，若後續能成功由 OSAT 及先進封裝延伸至前段
Fab ，單一客戶需求量與設備價值將進一步放大。 產品面， 2027 年底
OHT 產能目標 2,500~3,000 台，對應月產能約 250 台，較先前月產能
100 台大幅成長 ；EFEM 產能目標 3,000 台，Stocker 500~800 台，AMR 
800~1,000 台， 整體 產能 規模 將明顯 放大 。 海外 市場 也是為 下一 階段
成長動能，目前有 9.3 億元訂單於海外執行。  
機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期 ：機器人方面，盟英進度
持續 推進， 目前北美客戶已開始小量出貨，年底諧波減速機產能目標
30 萬顆以上， 2027 年有望進一步提升至 80 萬顆。現階段仍處於量產
初期，固定成本、折舊與設備投入較高，產量需達到數十萬顆以上規
模才較容易跨過損平點。機器人 2026 年仍偏向小量貢獻， 2027 年開
始觀察損平與放量進度， 2028 年後 將成為第二成長曲線。 我們預估 盟
立 2026/2027 年營收達 98.5 /146.7 億元、EPS  2.51 /6.8 元，給予 「買進」
評等，目標價 238 元(2027 年 EPSx 35 倍)。 
風險因子： 半導體客戶擴產遞延 、機器人產品競爭加劇 。

### Page 2

個股報告

2 0 2 6 / 09 / 17

2

盟立成立於 1989 年，為台灣主要自動化 系統整合廠商之一，營運模式以「系
統整合 (SI)+ 自 有設 備 」為 核 心 ， 業務 涵 蓋半 導體 、 面 板 、物 流 及機 器人 等 領
域。近年公司策略明顯聚焦半導體自動化市場，主要產品包括空中走行式搬
送系統 (OHT) 、晶圓 /載 板 傳 送 設 備(EFEM) 、 晶 圓 儲 存 設 備(Stocker) 及 無 人 搬
運車 (AMR/AGV) 。 
生產據點方面，公司目前於竹科、銅鑼、中科及南科均設有生產基地，其中
竹科以機器人相關產品為主，銅鑼及南科負責 OHT ，中科則生產 Stocker 及
EFEM 。隨半導體客戶持續擴廠，公司近年積極進行產品規格標準化及生產流
程優化，包括 OHT 機種收斂、軌道規格統一，以及軟體平台模組化，有利後
續由專案型生產逐步轉向標準化、規模化製造，降低單位生產成本並提升交
付效率。我們認為，盟立在半導體自動化市場的核心競爭優勢，在於產品線
完整及軟硬體整合能力 。半導體廠內 OHT、EFEM、Stocker 及 AMR 之間需高
度整合，設備導入後具一定系統黏著度，其中 OHT、EFEM 與 AMR 等設備在
資訊串聯及搬運指令整合上具有較高連動性，有利盟立由單一設備進一步擴
大整體專案供應範圍。  
除半導體設備外，公司也積極布局機器人產業。旗下盟英科技主要投入諧波
減速機及關節模組，並透過 傲威科技向整機機器人領域延伸。 2026 年公司與
精確、敏實集團合資成立傲威科技，由盟立提供技術與產品、敏實集團負責
結構件，進一步強化機器人整機整合能力。  
圖表一、盟立產能配置

資料來源：盟立，中信投顧整理  
AI 伺服器、 HPC 及先進封裝需求持續成長，使全球半導體資本支出進入新一
輪擴張週期。本輪擴產範圍涵蓋晶圓代工、記憶體、先進封裝、封測及載板
等領域，同步帶動廠內自動化搬運設備需求。  
台積電 ，2026 年 Capex 已上修至 600 ～640 億美元 ，公司更宣布額外投資 1,000
億美元用以在美新建 4 座新廠 ；也規劃台灣未來數年將興建高達 13 座先進製
程及先進封裝廠房 。我們預估台積電 2026/2027/2028 年全球合計在建案量分
別達 16/16/5 座， 2026~2028 年 Capex 合計突破 2,000 億美元。

圖表二、 台積電各廠區興建時程表

資料來源：台積電，中信投顧整理  
封測產業進入擴產週期，受惠 AI 需求強勁及台積電先進封裝產能快速擴張，

mainly comprising of high-end equipment. Management expects revenue in 1H26 and

(7) To meet with the strong demand, the company will expand capacity to reach 396

remains strong, with up to 120 additional units/year.

laminators (mentioned in 4 above), b) FOPLP vacuum laminators, and c) wafer-level

### Page 3

We see several indicators suggesting strong ABF equipment demand, including (1) the 
lead time for ABF vacuum laminators has extended into 2031, implying ~1.5k units of 
mid-to-high end equipment orders (assuming UTR is full for Eternal Precision’s capacity 
in both Japan and China throughout 2027-31), vs. ~600 units of total equipment 
shipped during 2022-25. This suggests demand for ABF equipment is strengthening 
(even stronger than previous upcycle in 2020-22) and shifting toward higher-end 
(management mentioned that the four major Taiwanese ABF customers mainly ordered 
the 90-ton 3-stage vacuum laminators for new capacity expansions), (2) Eternal 
Precision has seen some customers voluntarily offer to pay a premium to secure 
equipment supply and shorten the long lead time, a trend that was not observed by the 
company during the last upcycle, and (3) contract liabilities uptrend has just started 
(Exhibit 2), and we expect to see even stronger growth in coming years considering the 
upcoming ABF substrate industry capacity expansion trend (from 2027 to at least 2031, 
Exhibit 3) 
The extended order lead time for the key ABF equipment supports our positive view on 
the ABF substrate industry. ABF suppliers remain proactive in capacity expansion, but 
demand continues to outpace supply, thus we expect the shortage ratio of ABF 
substrates could reach 14%/34%/51% in 2H26/2027/2028 (see here). We see limited 
risk from capacity expansion by China ABF suppliers due to their limited access to new 
ABF equipment. Eternal Precision has seen some China ABF customers offering 
premiums to shorten equipment lead time, but the company mentioned that they 
prioritize major customers, as well as those with secured ABF film supply (we continue to 
see limitations on ABF film supply for China ABF suppliers). Meanwhile, more 
end-customers are asking key ABF suppliers for capacity beyond 2029, consistent with 
the fact that major ABF suppliers have better access to key ABF equipment. 
In terms of future substrate products, Unimicron previously mentioned that MP 
shipment of EMIB-T substrates could start in 2027 (see here). While Japanese ABF 
suppliers will be the first to have access to the new ABF vacuum laminators for EMIB-T 
substrates given their better production yields, Unimicron is tracking close by targeting a 
50% yield for this new product. For the more advanced glass core substrates, we 
continue to see Kinsus and Unimicron making progress with this new technology (see 
here and here), while commercialization is unlikely in the near term (beyond 2029 per 
GSe). Overall, we believe that the most advanced packaging substrates could become a 
key revenue/GM driver in the longer term given their better pricing/margin profile (most 
advanced packaging substrates typically have GM of 60%+ upon MP vs. 
Kinsus/Unimicron GM at 26.1%/24.8% as of 2Q26). 
Maintain our Buy ratings on NYPCB (on CL) and Kinsus, but stay Neutral on Unimicron, 
with unchanged 12m TPs for NYPCB/Kinsus/Unimicron of 
NT$2,500/NT$1,125/NT$1,220.  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy-rated on NYPCB 
16 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan ABFFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 27/41% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 1H25), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s

high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (122%

2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s
earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,500 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)

Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.
Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~106% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,125 for Kinsus is based on

16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We are Neutral rated on the
shares given long-term growth opportunity prospects: (1) over 70% of ABF shipments
are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and makes
price hike less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker than

peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its ability to
capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing more
favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see better pricing outlook
to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF account for 10% of the
company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers due to the
company continuing to lose market share to peers given its less favourable production

yield and slow capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our
outlook for limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally
positive view in the long term on its profitability trajectory.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,220 for Unimicron is
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E

(10.3x).
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 056: 260918_daiwa_ Jentech

Source PDF: all report pdf/260918_daiwa_ Jentech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We received an update from Jentech on 18 September. Its 
share price has been negatively impacted week to date by market talk 
concerning the adoption of removable lids in Vera Rubin and Rubin Ultra. 
However, we maintain our positive view on its ongoing product-upgrade 
roadmap and expect the ASP-upgrade trend to continue in the following 
years. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, Record-high 
monthly revenue for August; 3Q26 QTD revenue tracking ahead, on 7 
September 2026).

What’s the impact: Promising ASP upgrade trend. Due to rising thermal 
design power (TDP), we expect the ASP of Jentech’s removable lid for 
Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra to be 5-6x higher vs. that of the current one-
piece lid. Following better-than-expected shipments during the ramp-up of 
Vera Rubin, we now forecast its 3Q26 revenue at TWD9.65bn (previously 
TWD9.26bn vs. Bloomberg consensus of TWD9.62bn) and 4Q26 revenue 
at TWD10.62bn (previously TWD10.20bn, vs. Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD10.32bn). We believe there could be some revenue upside potential, 
subject to the adoption of removable lids for Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra. We 
now forecast an EPS CAGR of 86.1% over 2026-28E, from 78.8% 
previously, as we believe further product upgrades and a favourable 
product mix should continue to support improving profitability.

Bright outlook for GPU projects as well as ASIC projects. We believe 
Jentech has a more promising product roadmap for ASIC clients, with 
potential ASP upgrades from the current stiffener. After GPU clients adopt 
removable lids, it also could encourage its ASIC clients to adopt removable 
lids, translating to 15-20x ASP increase from here. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 6-15%, mainly as a result of our more aggressive revenue and 
gross-/operating-margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD9,420 (from TWD6,770), still based on a target PER of 73x (vs. its 
past-3-year PER range of 17-62x) applied to our 1-year forward EPS 
forecast, which is rolled over to 4Q26E-3Q27E (from 3Q26E-2Q27E). We 
believe our target valuation is fair, given Jentech’s strong earnings visibility 
and leading position in chip-level thermal solutions, which we believe 
warrant a rerating in addition to its strong moat and competitive landscape 
in the segment. Downside risks: weaker-than-expected global AI server 
demand and slower-than-expected thermal spec upgrades.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-10% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more aggressive in our revenue and gross-
/operating-margin assumptions.

18 September 2026

3Q26 preview: stronger-than-expected spec upgrades

➢ We maintain our positive view on Jentech’s product roadmap 
➢ Likely EPS upward revision trend in the following months 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12-month TP to TWD9,420

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Jentech Precision Industrial is a leading global thermal-solutions and precision-
metal components maker that serves leading semiconductor and server vendors. As of 2025, its 
revenue comprised thermal heat spreaders (73%), lead frames (9%), electronic components (7%), 
communication connectors (1%) and others (10%).

### Page 5

Jentech Precision Industrial (3653 TT): 18 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 29 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Jentech is the same individual, Mr. Zhao Zongxin. We note that Mr.
Zhao has extensive long-term experience in senior management within the company and across
its subsidiaries, which is a positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 1
Between 2021-25, Jentech has maintained a dividend payout ratio of around 60%, which we
consider reasonable for balancing shareholder returns with reinvestment needs. This consistent
approach reflects prudent capital management discipline and a stable financial policy.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company engages in transactions with subsidiaries primarily for normal operational
purposes, such as purchase of materials and components. The proportion of these costs to
overall COGS remains low, and no irregular or non-transparent transactions were identified.
E Supply chain
management 1
Jentech has a structured procurement process, including supplier evaluation, signature of
procurement contracts, and requirements for compliance with environmental and safety
regulations. The company also diversifies sourcing channels (major suppliers from Taiwan,
Japan, Europe, Korea, USA) and maintains stable supply relationships, with clear
communication protocols for prompt response to supplier issues. This reflects strong supply
chain transparency and robust quality control measures.
E Data security 1
The company has established an information security team, conducts annual internal control
audits, and implements procedures such as asset inventory, network security monitoring, virus
prevention systems, firewall management, and access rights control. Backup systems and
disaster recovery mechanisms are in place, allowing the company to restore information system
operations and minimize damage during major incidents or equipment failures.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
TFT-LCD Panel Prices for Sep-
26: TVs, Monitors and NBs Flat
MoM
Chinese panel makers are planning to raise panel prices in the
coming months to mitigate the cost pressure, which we believe
could find some resistance among their brand customers. Glass
opportunities in advanced packaging remain the key reasons that
major panel makers are trading at 1.5-2.0x P/B.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices stayed flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices all stood flat for the month of September,  in line with our
expectations. While the overall TV panel demand was lackluster, there is still some
seasonal demand as brands are preparing for year-end promotions. Recently, the
major Chinese panel makers have notified their customers of price hikes from

September/October in order to mitigate increasing margin pressure caused by rising
costs of various components, including driver ICs, glass substrates and PCB.
Although major Chinese panel makers might leverage their annual maintenance of
three to seven days during October 2026 as a bargaining chip, we think it might take
more negotiations with brand customers before they can really raise panel prices,
since order momentum will slow down in the coming months. On a quarterly

average basis, TV panel prices  declined in the low-single-digits % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices were flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
stayed flat MoM during the month. Both applications continues to  see slower
demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about  increasing
component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and reluctant to
given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect IT panel

prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think that the current valuation of Taiwanese panel
makers, AUO (2409.TW) and Innolux (3481.TW) both at 1.7x P/B, has already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging for revenue contribution in
2028. However, some supply chain players think this might take longer, and there
seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying for this opportunity. In
China, while expectations are also building for the glass core substrate, we have a

relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.5x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.8x P/B),
on its scale advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Current valuation of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in the expectation of glass
in advanced packaging from 2H28.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition are worth monitoring.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down from 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

### Page 4

Idea
4
support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.8x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 071: 260922_ubs_GUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260922_ubs_GUC.pdf

Date: 2026-09-22

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 22 September 2026
Global Research
Global Unichip
Better wafer allocation and demand upside
driving H226-2027E earnings growth
Better wafter allocation driving H226-2027E CPU project ramps
We expect CPU projects to remain GUC's main revenue driver in 2026-27E and see
improved wafer allocation into 2027E. We now expect Google CPU volumes to ramp
from 1.3mn units in 2026E to ~4mn units in 2027E, supported by rising TPU shipments
(up 117% YoY in 2027E), a rising CPU-to-TPU ratio, and better wafer allocation from

TSMC. Although GUC's CPU projects carry lower GM, we believe they remain earnings
accretive given the operating leverage from limited incremental OPEX requirements. We
now expect 2026E revenue growth of 94% YoY to NT$66.3 bn (vs prior +90% YoY) and
2027E revenue to grow 146% YoY (with Google CPU contributing 67% of 2027E sales),
up from our prior estimate of 84% YoY. We also expect OPM to expand from 13.2% in
2026E to 13.9% in 2028E. Accordingly, we raise our 2027/28E EPS estimates by

29%/22% and lift our PT to NT$8,660 (from NT$6,700), based on 52x average 2027-
28E EPS. Our 2027/28E EPS estimates are 23%/14% above consensus.
Broadening CSP exposure supports medium-term growth
Beyond the Google CPU ramp, we also see improving demand from Microsoft's Maia
platform, where GUC supplies CPU and the Maia 200 accelerator. Although Maia 300 is
shifting to another supplier, we see extended runway for Maia 200, as well as continued
CPU shipments into 2027E. For the next-gen Maia 400, we believe this remains in the

bidding stage, with GUC maintaining a competitive position over peers. GUC also
secured a 2nm ASIC project from Meta in Q226 for a turnkey revenue ramp from mid-
2028E. These CPU and ASIC projects, along with GUC's strong execution track record,
provide attractive opportunities to engage with top CSPs on accelerator opportunities.
Nevertheless, we do not expect meaningful revenue contribution before 2028.
Automotive to see more significant contribution from mid-2027E

We believe automotive will be another key growth driver for GUC over the medium
term, initially supported by a Chinese EV customer from late-2026, followed by a US EV
maker in H227E. We estimate automotive will contribute 2%/13% of total revenue in
2026E/27E, respectively, increasing further to 18% in 2028E. As GUC offers higher
value-add in the automotive projects vs. Google CPU, we believe they should support
GM and deliver a higher profit contribution relative to sales. In addition, revenue could

see further upside should these solutions expand into robotic applications.
Valuation: Retain BUY, raise PT to NT$8,660
Our NT$8,660 price target is based on 52x average 2027-28E EPS (vs prior 50x), vs
historical 17-84x range, supported by 56% CAGR in earnings over 2025-30E. We
reiterate our Buy rating, as GUC is well positioned for upside in CPU demand, ramp of
automotive projects, other ASICs and emerging optical interconnect opportunities.

### Page 2

Global Unichip UBS ResearchGlobal Unichip (3443.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can GUC further grow its AI and high-performance computing sales?
Yes, in our view, supported by multiple drivers. GUC has benefited from being part of TSMC's
ecosystem, with better access to TSMC's technology roadmap, especially on advanced nodes. It has
won several CPU/XPU projects from US hyperscalers and it is also working with leading EV makers on

next-generation autonomous driving ASICs. GUC aims to expand its tier-1 hyperscaler customer base
and we believe there is an opportunity for it to provide advanced packaging services, leveraging the
CoWoS capacity it secured, even though it is not directly involved in the ASIC design.
Q: Are GUC's projects on mature nodes (16nm and above) sustainable?
We think they are likely to remain stable. GUC's customers are sticky on 16nm and higher nodes.
Owing to its strong IP portfolio, which reduces design costs and improves manufacturing yield, these

mature nodes still contribute around 30% of GUC's sales in 2025. While we expect mature nodes to
remain accretive to margins – catering to repeat projects that require little R&D resources – the
growth is likely to be modest at 27% CAGR for 2025-28E vs 104% CAGR for advanced nodes.
Q: Will GUC's OPM recover in 2026-28?
We expect its OPM to show a slight improvement in 2026, as it further ramps the lower-GM Google
CPU project, and incurs a temporary increase in opex due to the new Turing centre. However, we

foresee a more significant improvement in OPM from 2027 as it ramps a higher GM autonomous
driving project from H227, growing  NRE contributions, and restocking of mature projects.
We reiterate our Buy rating on GUC and raise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 0.4%/29%/22%, respectively,
primarily reflecting higher sales contributions from Google's CPU projects. We raise our price target
from NT$6,700 to NT$8,660 based on 52x average 2027-28E EPS (vs a 17-84x historical range),

supported by a 2025-30E EPS CAGR of 56%. Potential catalysts include: 1) further TPU/CPU upside
from the Google project; 2) the continued ramp of Microsoft's AI CPU/ASIC chips and the potential to
win back future-gen projects; 3) upside on new 2nm project wins; 4) a faster ramp of autonomous EV
projects; and 5) advanced NRE opportunities with higher dollar content.
GUC's revenue remained muted in 2023-24, as it shifted its resources from Chinese to US customers

due to BIS export controls. The customer diversification effort began bearing fruit in H125, as GUC
recorded a notable uptick in the turnkey sales to US crypto customers, while the Google CPU ramp
since late Q425 appears sustainable. With the support of its major shareholder TSMC, which has
enabled better access to advanced technology, GUC continues to work with Microsoft, Tesla and
other hyperscalers on custom AI chipsets, which should underpin its  growth in the next few years.

GUC stock rallied 57% in the past three months vs 3% for the TAIEX, on Google TPU news in our
view, and is now trading at 55x 2027E EPS vs its historical 17-84x range. We believe the stock should
continue to re-rate on TPU upside, rising CPU/TPU ratio, multiple automotive project ramps, and
potential upside from wafer allocation. We expect  a 56% CAGR in earnings over 2025-30E and
foresee potential upward revisions in consensus earnings estimates being a catalyst.

GUC is an IC design service company founded in 1998. The company's business is to design chipsets
for its customers who do not have semiconductor capability. It is ~35% owned by TSMC.

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is skewed (1.7:1) to the upside
GUC is trading at NT$7,615 (as of 21 September 2026).

UPSIDE (NT$12,000.00): Our upside scenario factors in stronger sales growth in 2027-
28E than we forecast in our base case, on the back of better demand for Google's TPU
and US/China automotive projects, more N3 wafer allocation by leading foundries, and
faster penetration of other hyperscalers, lifting 2027/28E sales by 170%/40% YoY. Our
upside OPM assumptions are 13.4%/14.1% for 2027/28E, 0.2ppt above our base case
owing to a limited increase in opex, supporting a 96% CAGR in earnings over 2026-28E.

Our upside scenario valuation of NT$12,000.00 implies a 76x 2027E PE.
BASE (NT$8,660.00): Our base case reflects our expectations of a solid business growth
outlook in 2027-28E, supported by the continued ramp of Google's CPU project, initial
ramp of automotive projects from Q426, and expansion into other hyperscalers. We
forecast 2027/28E sales to grow 146%/34% YoY, and OPM to expand to 13.2%/13.9%
from 12.7% in 2025 on operating leverage, despite its GM potentially normalising to

around 17.8% in 2028 (from 24.8% in 2025) on a higher proportion of sales from the
CPU project, resulting in earnings growth of 83% CAGR over 2026-28E. Our base-case
valuation of NT$8,660.00 implies a 52x 2027-28E average PE.
DOWNSIDE (NT$5,000.00): Our downside scenario factors in slower sales growth in
2027/28E than we forecast in our base case, due to softer growth in Google's CPU due to
a wafer capacity constraint and slower demand for mature process products, dampening

sales growth to 121%/26% YoY. Our downside OPM assumptions are 13.0%/13.7% for
2027/28E, 0.2pp below our base case, resulting in earnings growth decelerating to a
68% CAGR over 2026-28E. Our downside scenario valuation of NT$5,000.00 implies a
41x 2027E P/E.

Forecast returns

Company Description
Global Unichip (GUC) is a leading IC design service company in Taiwan, founded in 1998. The
company's business is to design chipsets for customers that do not have IC design capability,
with the goal of reducing entry barriers and risks for chipset design and shortening the time

to market. The company's target to enable more IC design for start-ups and system
companies is complimentary with TSMC, which would benefit from a growing customer and
application base, in addition to the company's existing IC design and IDM customers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Global Unichip based on a PE methodology.
Downside risks include a slower-than-expected business recovery for mature nodes, lack of
new project wins, and wafer capacity constraints.

### Page 9

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? Kinsus (3189.TW, not covered) reported August 2026 profit per TWSE
regulatory requirements:
• August revenue was NT$4,810M (+6% m/m, +41% y/y). July and August
revenues accounted for 68% of the consensus 3Q revenue estimate.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$1,110M (+256% y/y), 51% of the consensus 3Q
pre-tax profit forecast.
• August net profit was NT$817M (+354% y/y), 55% of the consensus 3Q net

profit estimate.
Our thoughts:
Kinsus is one of the leading global IC substrate suppliers, with exposure to both ABF
and BT substrates and the highest BT revenue mix among Taiwanese substrate
peers. We see an encouraging read-through to Unimicron and NYPCB, both rated
OW, to the extent that the strength reflects improving underlying substrate
profitability. The results are consistent with our constructive view of ABF substrate
makers and continued margin improvement.

With one month’s net profit equivalent to more than half of consensus quarterly
earnings, the disclosure suggests upside risk to Kinsus’ 3Q consensus estimates.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)

Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand

n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.1x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Idea
Current debate: Can MediaTek meet Street 2028 forecasts given Intel EMIB-T
supply issue? Intel's EMIB-T substrate supply has been a potential bottleneck for
future TPU v9 ("Humufish") shipments in 2028, making some investors reluctant to
own MediaTek. The issue stems from a supply-demand mismatch. We forecast 3mn
TPU v9 units, as demand from Google (covered by Brian Nowak) remains strong.
However, as discussed in our AI Supply Chain Tracker, we expect only 2mn EMIB-T

substrates, i.e. 40–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028. This aligns with
Digitimes' report that Google is considering CoWoS as a backup for its 2027 TPU on
lagging EMIB-T substrate yields.  For TSMC, supporting CoWoS could also help
regain market share from EMIB-T. Our checks indicate that both MediaTek and
TSMC are engaging to meet customer (Google) demand.
TSMC's willingness to support production increases confidence in our aggressive

2028 EPS forecast: Assuming TSMC CoWoS-L can support 0.5mn TPU v9 units and
EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units, our 3mn-unit target for 2028 appears highly
achievable, with a higher ASP from the CoWoS version. CoWoS-L carries a
structurally higher cost, and the RDL (redistribution layer) interposer connection
requires tape-out of three additional photomask layers. However, our checks also
suggest that Google is willing to pay these incremental costs to secure dual

sourcing, which should help MediaTek sustain its gross margin.
New debate: Who could be the second CSP customer? Investors have asked
whether MediaTek could win Tesla's AI6 edge AI chip program. We believe AI6 is
better suited to GUC, given its 10%–25% margin profile. Considering the higher tech
content of MediaTek's design services, incl. IP + design capabilities, we believe
MediaTek targets a 30%–35% margin profile. Instead, we see a growing probability

that MediaTek wins a cloud AI ASIC project from an AI-focused CSP and/or AI lab
customer through its  Nvidia / NVLink Fusion partnership. In previous notes, we
highlighted interest from both SpaceX and an AI lab customer. The customer's
decision could come in 4Q26 and serve as a significant catalyst for MediaTek. With
TPU upside and a potential new project, we expect MediaTek AI ASIC-related
revenue of US$13.5bn and US$54bn in 2027 and 2028, resp., representing c.38% and

72% of total revenue. Reiterate Top Pick.

### Page 2

2
TSMC CoWoS capacity support eases concerns regarding
MediaTek 2028  TPU production
Smartphone market still lukewarm into 2027, but can serve as a long-term option
value: At our recent China Best Conference, meetings involving China smartphone OEM
and NAND module makers appeared to indicate China's smartphone demand may not
recover in 2027, as mobile DRAM prices may not be falling anytime soon. We expect
MediaTek’s 3Q26 revenue to be ahead of guidance because of customer pull-in to avoid

the SoC price hike rather than due to demand improvement.
That said, once AI smartphones become a killer application, MediaTek should rank among
the best-positioned semiconductor companies to provide the edge computing capabilities
required in handsets. We also believe 2028 should be a strong year for MediaTek's
smartphone SoC business.
1. Google TPU supply chain update
TSMC CoWoS may  support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek TPU
in 2028: In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but

Cloud AI taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that  MediaTek may secure a portion of
CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure minimum volume production. But, we believe it
will use EMIB-T from Intel (covered by Joseph Moore)  as the primary solution to reduce
costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well. Our recent industry checks
suggest TSMC CoWoS would continue to support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm
(Humufish) MediaTek TPU in 2028. We now assume around 500k units of 2nm TPU going

through CoWoS production as a second source. The 3nm TPU in 2028 of around 2mn
units is higher than our initial expectation of 1mn units. We therefore raise our MediaTek
2028 TPU revenue assumption to US$51.5bn.
2nm TPU TAM is supported by different SKU: At our AI Summit, MediaTek management
stated that its design service is capable of both training and inference design. Recently,
investors have been asking whether MediaTek is involved in the design of 2nm TriggerFish

TPU, intended for AI inference. Our supply chain checks suggest this could be true. The
TriggerFish chip has more features (I/O die upgrade, one more simulation die, and change
of HBM controller to HBM4e), so we think MediaTek’s 2nm TPU blended ASP could go
higher. We estimate ASP at US$12-20k (we now model US$13k). But again, the EMIB-T
substrate yield should be the key risk to volume.
CoWoS constraint eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU bull

case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s 180k CoWoS-S
booking implies ~3.6mn units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original 3mn
shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Who could be the second and third customer for MediaTek
in AI ASICs?
NVLink Fusion partnership should be the key catalyst for MediaTek to win new
projects:
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server systems.
In our report, MediaTek: NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC; Stay OW, Top Pick (31 Aug 2026), we shared that MediaTek had

maintained its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share guidance despite the broader partnership,
noting that commercialization would take time. However, we believe the partnership
materially increases MediaTek's chances of winning a second CSP customer.
Overall, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumptions, driven by: 1) a higher
probability of winning a second or even third CSP customer; 2) greater revenue from
material preparation; and 3) a strengthened partnership with Alphabet on TPU programs.

In today's research, we incorporate potential revenue contributions from new customers.
Exhibit 2: NVLink Fusion to support connection with custom ASIC
Source: Nvidia
MediaTek's second CSP customer could begin contributing revenue from subsystem
builds in 2028: We believe the subsystem would include procurement of key components
such as HBM and ABF substrates, as well as wafer foundry capacity prepayments.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Our checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for a second CSP customer
rather than supplying the XPU itself. We now expect an initial revenue contribution in
2028, based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and
an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across

both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross
margins at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale. We continue to
expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business unit's 15–20%.

3. Why TSMC is motivated  to support MediaTek and Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost due to its larger reticle size, as
we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB
competition (14 Apr 2026). We believe TSMC also wants to address growing competition
from Intel's EMIB-T, which we estimate will reach 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity 2028,

implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our checks suggest that Google is willing to absorb the
incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which should allow MediaTek to sustain
its gross margin.

Average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale to 15-
20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is
converted for 12-inch wafer equivalent, which we believe would be much easier to

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
communicate with customers compared  with TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We now expect  Intel EMIB-T could
produce close to 2.5mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5 chips per
wafer of EMIB-T, while TSMC CoWoS could support around 500k units.
We see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly decrease

to 95kwpm in 2027 as Intel is converting some capacities toward EMIB-T.

Raise price target from NT$5,588 to NT$6,188
We continue to derive our price target (our base-case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change mostly reflects our earnings estimate changes. All
of  our key valuation assumptions remain unchanged: cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2,
risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%), intermediate growth rate of 12%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values rise from NT$7,675 to NT$8,500 and  NT$2,845 to

NT$3,150, respectively.

Morgan Stanley Research 9
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Vanguard International Semi. (5347.TWO)

Vanguard International Semi.
Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$185 is based on 25x 2027E P/E, which we see as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth
momentum and increasing outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash dividend policy. Our
target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.6x/3.5x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along with higher costs due to inflation or more severe

ASP competition from Chinese peers; 2) lower UTR of the new capacity at the newly acquired fab, with depreciation burden;
and 3) less-than-expected outsourcing opportunities from IDMs.
NXP Semiconductors NV
(NXPI.O; US$238.08; 1; 25 Sep 26; 16:00)
Valuation
Our price target of $370 is based on a 17x P/E applied to CY28 EPS, given peer multiple expansion tied to rising data center
exposure.
Risks
End Market: NXP derives roughly 55% of sales from the automotive end market and roughly 18% of sales from the industrial

end market. Therefore, any major changes in auto sales or industrial activity could result in upside/downside to our estimates
and target price for NXP.
Competition: NXP competes with several companies in analog, digital, and microcontrollers. Consequently, any changes in
market share could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Inventory Risk: NXP sells roughly 50-60% of its products through distributors. As a result, we believe any major fluctuations of

inventory in the distribution channel could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Macroeconomic: NXP’s geographic exposure spans multiple geographies including the U.S., Europe, and Asia. As a result, any
prolonged macroeconomic strength/weakness could result in upside/downside to our estimates and target price.
TSMC
(2330.TW; NT$2475.0; 1; 24 Sep 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$3,800 is based on 25x the average of our 2027E EPS, higher than its three-year

average forward PER, versus global peers’ average of 15-23x. We believe our PER target is justified by TSMC's leading position
in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook. Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor
foundry industry, we believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less vulnerable than peers to a
potential global economic downturn. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of 34x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of 13x/10x.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target price include: 1) weakness in the global
semiconductor market; 2) larger-than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost hikes and strong NTD; 3)
competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a
slowdown in demand either from a global economic downturn or a global trade disruption triggered by tariffs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

陽明 (2609 TT) 
旺季獲利上修，淡季運價有撐

永豐觀點 
亞洲港口擁擠、船舶繞航、巴拿馬運河水位低下降低供給周轉支撐淡季運價有撐。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 3Q 美洲線運價優於預期旺季獲利佳，巴拿馬運河水位低下支撐 4Q
運價相對高檔，(2) 亞洲線進入旺季相對暢旺，(3) 歷史 PBR 落在 0.5-4.3 倍，目
前 PBR 為 0.54 倍 ， 評價處於相對低檔 ， 維持買進 ， 目標價由71 元(0.7X2026BVPS)
調整到 73 元(0.65 X2027 BVPS)。

ESG 評析 
陽明企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

2

陽明為全球第九大海運業者：陽明海運是全球第九大航商，2026/9 運力 77.3 萬
TEU 約 99 艘(較 2025 年底的 71.4 萬 TEU 成長 8.3%)，市佔率 2.2%，加入 THE 
Alliance 聯盟 (聯盟成 員之 一赫伯羅特2025/1 退出 ，聯盟更名為 Premier 
Alliance(2025/2-2030/1)。安裝脫硫塔比重超過 6 成，自有櫃佔 54%。1H26 年
美洲線佔營收比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，其他航線(中東、南美等)
佔 12%，運送量 223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012 美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。
股權方面，官股持有 31%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元：受惠美洲線與亞洲線運價上漲，加上 LNG 船投
入地中海航線 ， 預估營收656 億元(+56%YoY)， 稅後獲利187.5 億元(+210%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元。4Q 歐洲線進入淡季、美洲線受巴拿馬運河水位降低相對有
撐、亞洲進入旺季加上塞港相對強勢，公司認為4Q 呈現緩步修正，研究部預估營
收 573 億元(+53%YoY)，稅後獲利 136.4 億元(+496%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 3.91
元。 
燃油成本的影響：陽明 1H26 年燃油成本佔 16%，6 月油價攀升至 692 美元/噸，
整體燃油成本較去年同期+1%。2Q 高硫油平均 617 美元/噸(+23.5%QoQ)，低硫
油平均 775 美元/噸(+24.5%QoQ)，因地緣政治影響，3Q 高硫油平均 562 美元/
噸-9%QoQ)，低硫油平均 792 美元/噸(+2.3%QoQ)，研究部假設陽明年耗油量
142 萬噸，船用燃油平均一年漲 100 美元/噸，燃油成本增加 44 億元，EPS 影響
1.26 元，不過航商透過燃油附加費的徵收以分散燃油成本上漲的壓力。

2026 年供給成長是近幾年最低一年：巴拿馬運河當局 9/4 將每天通行艘樹從 36
艘降至 34 艘，9/15 再降到 32 艘，目前運河水位較去年低 2.5%，預期 10 月水位
將低於去年同期 3.8%，艙位供給將再度限縮。無論是 Drewry，Clarkson 或是
Alphaliner 預估 2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的 ， 加上紅海改道的船舶回
歸延遲，有效運力釋放再度放緩，2026 年是供給相對較小的一年。 
3Q26 第 2 艘雙燃料船交船：新船在手訂單 23 艘約 30 萬 TEU，1Q26-4Q26 年
將交 5 艘 1.56 萬 TEU LNG 船(已交 4 艘)，3Q28-2Q29 交 7 艘 1.6 萬 TEU LNG
雙燃料船，4Q28-1Q30 交 6 艘 8 千 TEU 甲醇雙燃料船，4Q28-3Q29 年交 6 艘
1.3 萬 TEU LNG 雙燃料船。轉投資光明海運則預計 3Q28-2Q29 交 4 艘 6.4 萬
DWT 散裝船。

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶已確定之 ASIC 合作案。欣銓 1H26 在取得不動產、廠房
及設備的現金流出達新台幣 70.07 億元，較 1H25 的 18.65 億元
大幅增加約 51.4 億元。預期 2H26 則隨龍潭廠機台陸續交付並
完成數據驗證後，進入產能爬坡期，營收逐步向上。在排除匯
率波動前提下 ， 獲利亦預計維持逐季成長的趨勢 。 預估欣銓2026
年稅後 EPS 9.94 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最重要的增
長引擎 。 公司與策略夥伴(晶圓廠 )合作的 ASIC 測試專案 07/2026
開始明顯增加，而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光
子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向上，
因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元 (2027 年 PER24X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元： AI 相關需求佳和通訊相關訂單維持強
勁。欣銓 2Q26 營收為 45.34 億元， QoQ+13.81% 。營收依應用
別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 33.4% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 18.2% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 12.2% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.5% ； Storage 佔 10.8% ； PC/Consumers 佔
9.9% ；Memory 佔 5.4% ；其他 1.6% 。毛利率的部份， 2Q26 毛利
率為 40.17% ，增加 1.81 個百分點。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元。  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與
進場裝機後，已於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓
08/2026 營收增加至 17.94 億元， MoM+1.57% ， YoY+46.57% 。
此廠區主要與晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 合作，針對  AI ASIC 提供
測試服務。這批高階機台目前的稼動率極高，幾乎處於滿載狀
態，其量產將顯著貢獻 3Q26 營收，也會讓晶圓代工業務的營收
佔比在 3Q26 逐漸放大。此外，在傳統應用領域如車用 IC 及  
General MCU 方面 ， 由於客戶端在疫後的庫存已降至合理水位 ，
需求正逐步回升。預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG （環境、社會、公司治理）相關政策與行動，並於官方
網站設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目
標。  
環境（ Environmental ）  
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi （科學基礎減碳目標倡議）承諾，強化減碳
目標與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會（ Social ）  
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理（ Governance ）  
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會（ BSI ）ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台
灣企業永續獎（ TCSA ）－永續報告獎金獎」，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊
揭露與社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議（ SBTi ）官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 21 日

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

展望 2H26，伺服器業務持續為主要成長動能，1H26 伺服器營收
占比已達 78%，全年占比有望進一步提升至 80%左右；受惠營收
規模擴大及產品組合優化，2H26 營業費用率預期降至 11-12%。
產品方面， 水冷業務1H26 營收占比已達 55%，Vera Rubin 及 T3
水冷專案預計 9 月開始量產，惟主要放量貢獻將落在 2027 年；
DIMM 水冷已於 2Q26 送樣 CSP 及企業型客戶，後續有望成為新
成長動能。氣冷業務則受惠一般型伺服器需求維持強勁，2H26 表
現有望優於 1H26。PC 及 VGA 則受終端需求疲弱影響，全年預估
衰退 10~15%，公司持續聚焦高階電競及商務型等高毛利產品。車
用業務則暫緩拓展，資源集中於伺服器市場。展望 2027 年，受惠
AI 伺服器水冷需求快速成長，整體營收預期年增四成以上，伺服
器營收占比維持 80%以上；隨高毛利水冷及客製化產品占比提
升，公司持續以毛利率逐年改善 2~3 個百分點為目標，產品組合
優化有利獲利能力持續提升。產品方面，Vera Rubin 及 T3 水冷
專案預計進入主要放量期，DIMM 水冷亦有望開始貢獻。CDU 方
面 ， 隨L2L 需求升溫 ， 憑藉無風扇設計優勢 ， 企業型In-Rack CDU
市占率目標達 50%以上， Infra 客戶則以 1.6MW In -Row 或
SideCar CDU 為主。氣冷方面，一般型伺服器及 ASIC 相關專案
能見度已延伸至 2027~2028 年，持續提供穩定營收及獲利貢獻；
PC、VGA 及車用等非核心產品占比則隨伺服器業務快速成長而進
一步下降 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估雙鴻2026 年營業收入為 414.40
億元(YoY +78.04%)，毛利率為 29.56%(YoY +2.18ppts)，營業利
益率為 17.78%(YoY +3.78ppts) ，稅後純益為 55.89 億元(YoY 
+117.30%)，EPS 為 60.05 元；2027 年營業收入為 581.88 億元(YoY 
+40.41%)，毛利率為 30.72%(YoY +1.16ppts) ，營業利益率為
19.49%(YoY +1.71ppts) ， 稅 後 純 益 為 87.49 億元 (YoY 
+56.54%)，EPS 為 94.00 元 。 公司近三年本益比區間為7~30 倍，
考量公司液冷產品比重持續增加帶動毛利率提升，研究部以 2027 
EPS 給予本益比 20 倍評價，目標價 1880 元，投資建議為買進。
